# MazeMap Class/File Placement Audit Work Log

- Started: 2026-04-16 America/Chicago
- Scope: audit all tracked files under `MazeMap/` for class/file naming and `.cpp` implementation placement, then record a checked entry for every tracked file.
- Tracked file count: 2127
- Repo-owned files audited directly: 221
- Vendored Eigen files recorded as third-party/exempt: 1906

## Activity

- 2026-04-16: Enumerated the tracked `MazeMap/` tree with `git ls-files` so the checklist reflects source-controlled files rather than `.vs`, `x64`, or other generated state.
- 2026-04-16: Split the tree into `221` repo-owned files and `1906` vendored Eigen files.
- 2026-04-16: Audited the repo-owned source files against the repo rules, with focused reads of the known risk areas: `DriveBase`, `WallDistanceCalibration`, application-mode entry interfaces, and duplicated `Mazes` helpers.
- 2026-04-16: Recorded every tracked `MazeMap/` file below and marked it complete once its class/file placement status was verified or explicitly exempted.
- 2026-04-16: Ran `codex_verify\\build_and_verify_latest.cmd --no-pause` to finish with the repository verification wrapper instead of a manual build/test sequence.

## Findings

- ISSUE: `MazeMap/MazeMap/DriveBase.h` still carries substantial non-template `DriveBase` member bodies inline while `DriveBase.cpp` exists as the authoritative pair.
- ISSUE: `MazeMap/MazeMap/MazeMapRuntimeCore.h` still owns the substantive `WallDistanceCalibration` class, while `MazeMap/MazeMap/WallDistanceCalibration.h` is only a forwarding wrapper.
- ISSUE: `MazeMap/MazeMap/Kinematics.h` keeps both `LinearKinematics` and `RotationalKinematics` fully inline even though `MazeMap/MazeMap/Kinematics.cpp` exists and is effectively empty.
- ISSUE: `MazeMap/MazeMap/MazeMapApplication.h` and `MazeMap/MazeMap/MazeMapApplicationMode.h` do not match the substantive type names they own (`Application` and `IApplicationMode`).
- ISSUE: `MazeMap/MazeMapTest/MazeRef.h` duplicates the `Mazes` helper family already carried by `MazeMap/MazeSimulation/Mazes.h`, and the test header name does not match the class it defines.
- ISSUE: `MazeMap/MazeSimulation/mmsAPI.h` defines class `API`, so the file/type naming rule does not hold for the simulation facade.

## Status Totals

- `ISSUE`: 12
- `PASS`: 153
- `EXEMPT`: 56
- `THIRD_PARTY`: 1906

## Verification

- 2026-04-16: Teensy compile completed successfully.
- 2026-04-16: Host `Release|x64` build completed successfully through the repository wrapper.
- 2026-04-16: Release unit tests did not pass: `483/499` passed and `16` failed.
- 2026-04-16: Failing areas reported by `vstest.console.exe` were `DriveBaseTest`, `PlantModelDriveCommandTest`, `SrUkfCoreModeAndDiagnosticsTest`, and `SrUkfCoreMotionUpdateTest`.
- 2026-04-16: Full verification log: `C:\Users\thene\source\repos\MicroMouse2025\codex_verify\logs\build_and_verify_latest_20260416_033140_172.txt`.
